			function loadDialog() {
				var sessionAlive = "1800";
				var notifyBefore = "300";
				setTimeout(function () {
					alertify.alert("You are about to be logged out due to inactivity, please press 'OK' to continue using ECEPP.")
						.setting({
							'onok': function () {
								$.get("/", function (data, status) { });
								clearTimeout(interval);
								loadDialog();
							},
						})
						.setHeader('Session Timeout');
					var interval = setTimeout(function () {
						$("body").append('<iframe src="/cas/logout" class="hide"><p>Your browser does not support iframes.</p></iframe>');
						$("body").append('<iframe src="/delta/logout.jsp" class="hide"><p>Your browser does not support iframes.</p></iframe>');
						alertify.alert("You have been logged out due to inactivity, please press 'OK' and then log back in to ECEPP.")
							.setting({
                                'onok': function () { window.location = '/delta/logout.jsp'; },
                                'onclose': function () { window.location = '/delta/logout.jsp'; }
							}).setHeader('Session Timeout');
					}, notifyBefore * 1000);
				}, (sessionAlive - notifyBefore) * 1000);
			};
			loadDialog();
				
			FilePond.registerPlugin(FilePondPluginFileValidateSize);
				
			$(document).ready(function() {
				$(".chosen").chosen();
			});
			$(function() {
				$(".chosen-select").chosen();
			});
		</script>

<script>
    moment().format();

<script>
$(document).ready(function(){
	
	var wordpressFormId = '';
	
	var table =$('#noticeResultsTable').DataTable( {
        dom: 'rtip',
        
        
  		"aoColumnDefs" : [
  							{
  								"visible" : false,
  								"aTargets" : [ 6 ]
  							},
  					  		{
  								"visible" : false,
  								"aTargets" : [ 7 ]
  							},  
  							{
  								"visible" : false,
  								"aTargets" : [ 8 ]
  							},
  							{
  								"visible" : false,
  								"aTargets" : [ 9 ]
  							},
  						  { 
	                             "orderData" : [7],
	                             "targets" : [3] 
	                      },
  						  { 
	                             "orderData" : [8],
	                             "targets" : [4] 
	                      }
  						 ],
	   "order": [[ 3, "desc" ]]
       
    } );
	
	
	 $('#keyword').on( 'keyup change', function () {
           if (table.search() !== this.value ) {
               table.search( this.value ).draw();
           } 
     } );
	 
	 $('#noticeType').on( 'change', function () {
         table.column(1).search( $(this).val() ).draw();
	 } );
	 
	 $('#currentState').on( 'change', function () {
         table.column(5).search($(this).val()).draw();
	 } );
	 
	
	 
    $('#noticeResultsTable thead tr:eq(0) th').each( function (i) {
    	if(i == 1){
         table.column( i ).data().unique().sort().each( function ( d, j ) {
        	 
        	 if(wordpressFormId != '' && d.toUpperCase() == ''.toUpperCase() ){
        		 $('#noticeType').append( '<option value="'+d+'" selected >'+d+'</option>' )
        		 table.column(1).search( '' ).draw();
        	 }else{
        		 $('#noticeType').append( '<option value="'+d+'">'+d+'</option>' )
        	 }
        	 
         } );
    	}
    	if(i == 5){
	         table.column( i ).data().unique().sort().each( function ( d, j ) {
	        	 
	        	 if(wordpressFormId != '' && d.toUpperCase() == ''.toUpperCase() ){
	        		 $('#currentState').append( '<option value="'+d+'" selected>'+d+'</option>' )
	        		 table.column(5).search('').draw();
	        	 }else{
	        		 $('#currentState').append( '<option value="'+d+'">'+d+'</option>' )
	        	 }
	        	 
	         } );
	    	}
    } );
	
    
    $.fn.dataTable.ext.search.push(
    		  function(settings, data, dataIndex) {
    			  if ($('#min-date').val() != "" && $('#max-date').val() != ""){
	    		    var min = moment($('#min-date').val(), 'DD-MM-YYYY').format('YYYYMMDD');
	    		    var max = moment($('#max-date').val(), 'DD-MM-YYYY').format('YYYYMMDD');
	    		    var publishedDate = moment(data[6] || 0, 'DD-MM-YYYY').format('YYYYMMDD'); // Our date column in the table
	    		    if (
	    		      (min == "" || max == "") ||
	    		      (moment(publishedDate).isSameOrAfter(min) && moment(publishedDate).isSameOrBefore(max))
	    		    ) {
	    		      return true;
	    		    }
	    		    return false;
	    		  }
    			  return true;
    		  }
    		);

	$('#min-date, #max-date').on( 'change', function(){
		if ($('#min-date').val() != "" && $('#max-date').val() != ""){
			table.draw();
		}
    } );
	
	
	
	if(wordpressFormId != ''){
		
		if('' != null && '' != ''){
			$('#keyword').val('');
			table.search( '' ).draw();
		}
	}
	
	
});
</script>
